local function Ips(Ipc,Ipl,Ipv,IpK,Ipu,Ipn)if#Ipc==0 then return Ipc end Ipv,IpK,Ipu,Ipn=Ipv or 1,IpK or 0,Ipu or 0,Ipn or 0 local IVI,IVS={},1 while IVS<=#Ipc do local IVj=Ipk(Ipl,Ipv,IpK,Ipu,Ipn)for IVX=1,0X10,1 do local IVO=IVj[IVX]for IVW=1,4,1 do if IVS>#Ipc then break end IVI[IVS]=IbH(Iby(Ibr(Ipc,IVS),IVO%0x100))IVO,IVS=IbG(IVO/0x100),IVS+1 end end Ipv=((Ipv+1))%IbT end return IbF(IVI)end local function IVk(IVJ,IVe,IVM)return Ipk(IVJ,0,IVe,IVM%IbT,IbG(IVM/IbT))end local function IVR(IVi,IVL,IVz,IVg,IVN)local IVY=IVk(IVN,IVz,IVg)local IVo={Iby(IVL,IVY[1])}for IVP=1,0x8,1 do IVo[IVP+1]=Iby(IVi[IVP],IVY[IVP+1])end IVo[10]=IVY[10]return IVo end local function IVZ(IVd,IVU,IVm,IVa)local IVE=IVk(IVa,IVU,IVm)return{Iby(IVd,IVE[1]);IVE[0Xa]}end local function IVC(IVB,IVx,IVD,IVG,IVr)local IVH=IVk(IVr,IVD,IVG)return IVB[2]==IVH[0Xa]and IVB[1]==Iby(IVx,IVH[1])end local function IVF(IVT,IVf,IVy,IVq,IVb)local IVp=IVk(IVb,IVy,IVq)if IVT[0xa]~=IVp[10]or IVT[1]~=Iby(IVf,IVp[1])then return nil end local IVV={}for IVt=1,8,1 do IVV[IVt]=Iby(IVT[IVt+1],IVp[IVt+1])end return IVV end local function IVw(IVh)local IVA={}for IVQ=1,#IVh,1 do local IVs=IVh[IVQ]IVA[IVQ]=IbH(IVs%0X100,IbG(IVs/0x100)%256,IbG(IVs/0x10000)%256,IbG(IVs/So[0Xc4])%0x100)end return IbF(IVA)end return{block=Ipk,crypt=Ips,seal=IVR,sealAdmission=IVZ,admit=IVC,resolve=IVF;packWords=IVw}end))()local IVl={So[197],So[0xc6],So[199],So[200];So[0xc9],So[0Xca],So[203];So[0xcc]}local function IVv(IVK,IVu,IVn)local ItI=(Sg(0X1a99ea3e))local ItS={}local Itj=SN[Sg(1144491152)][Sg(0x76b675f8)]for ItX=1,#ItI,1 do ItS[Itj(ItI,ItX)]=ItX-1 end local ItO=SN[Sg(1144491152)][Sg(0x706905b)]local ItW=SN[Sg(0x566e1ffc)][Sg(0X4bf261a0)]local Itk=SN[Sg(1144491152)][Sg(742187993)]local ItJ=SN[Sg(0x27571d63)][Sg(0X3730b8c4)]local Ite=SN[Sg(0x27571d63)][Sg(470198629)]local function ItM(ItR)return IVc[Sg(0X1cc922eb)](ItR,IVu,IVn)end if SN[Sg(0X37b81ac6)](IVK)~=(Sg(1144491152))then return IVK end local Iti=ItO(IVK)local ItL={}local Itz=1 while Itz<=Iti do local Itg=(Iti-Itz)+1 local ItN=Itg>=0x5 and 5 or Itg local ItY=0 local Ito=ItN>1 for ItP=0,0X4,1 do local ItZ if ItP<ItN then ItZ=ItS[Itj(IVK,Itz+ItP)]if not ItZ then Ito=false break end else ItZ=0x54 end ItY=ItY*0x55+ItZ end if Ito then local Itd=ItW(ItY/So[196])%0x100 local ItU=ItW(ItY/0x10000)%0x100 local Itm=ItW(ItY/0x100)%0X100 local Ita=ItY%256 if ItN==0b101 then ItJ(ItL,Itk(Itd,ItU,Itm,Ita))elseif ItN==4 then ItJ(ItL,Itk(Itd,ItU,Itm))elseif ItN==3 then ItJ(ItL,Itk(Itd,ItU))elseif ItN==0X2 then ItJ(ItL,Itk(Itd))end end Itz=Itz+ItN end return ItM(Ite(ItL))end local ItE={}local Itn=((function(ItC,ItB,Itx,ItD)local ItG=IVv(Itx,ItD)local Itr,ItH=SN[Sg(0X44378c90)][Sg(0x76b675f8)],SN[Sg(0X44378c90)][Sg(0x240fb2d6)]local function ItF(ItT,Itf)local Ity,Itq,Itb,Itp=Itr(ItT,Itf,Itf+3)return((Ity+Itq*256)+Itb*0X10000)+Itp*So[0Xc4]end local ItV={}local Itt,Itw=1,1 for Ith=1,#ItG,4 do local ItA=ItF(ItG,Ith)local ItQ=ItA%65536 local Its=SN[Sg(1450057724)][Sg(0X4bf261a0)](ItA/65536)ItV[ItQ]={Itt;Its;Itw}Itt,Itw=Itt+Its,Itw+1 end ItG=nil return function(Itc)local Itl=ItV[Itc]if not Itl then return nil end local Itv=ItH(ItC,Itl[1],(Itl[1]+Itl[0x2])-1)Itv=IVv(Itv,ItB,Itl[0x3])if#Itv~=0x8 and#Itv~=0X28 then return nil end local ItK={}for Itu=1,#Itv/0X4,1 do ItK[Itu]=ItF(Itv,((Itu-1))*0x4+1)end return ItK end
